{"forms":[{"form":"Euler-Lagrange equations","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A differential equation which describes a function mathbf q(t) which describes a stationary point of a functional, S( mathbf q)=∫L(t, mathbf q(t), mathbf ̇q(t)),dt, which represents the action of mathbf q(t), with L representing the Lagrangian. The said equation (found through the calculus of variations) is ∂L/∂ mathbf q=d/dt∂L/∂ mathbf ̇q and its solution for mathbf q(t) represents the trajectory of a particle or object, and such trajectory should satisfy the principle of least action."],"qualifier":"analytical mechanics","topics":["engineering","mechanical-engineering","mechanics","natural-sciences","physical-sciences"]}],"upstream_line":5233210,"upstream_offset":12185735229,"upstream_record_sha256":"02b3d71025f20a0e487d2d0cdaad932eb18005ac47a9a351b799a7662d199ee4","word":"Euler-Lagrange equation"}
